# This = player country; fromfrom = Fanatic Nation
country_event = {
	id = nations.6
	title = "nations.6.title"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_evt_announcement
	
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
				has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_exploding_planet
			}
		}
		text = "nations.6.desc"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
		}
		text = "nations.6.nuked.desc"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_exploding_planet
		}
		text = "nations.6.destroyed.desc"
	}
	trigger = {
		ROOT = {
			NOR = {
				any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_unified_planet_situation }
				has_technology = tech_primitive_unified_planet
			}
		}
		capital_scope = {
			OR = {
				is_planet_class = pc_nuked
				NOR = {
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_militarist_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_pacifist_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_spiritualist_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_blocker
					has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_blocker
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = { ROOT = { set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_unified_planet_situation_event_flag } }
	option = {
		name = "nations.6.destroyed"	
		trigger = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_exploding_planet
		}
		country_event = { id = PrimPlay_situation_event.3 days = 30 }
		capital_scope = {   
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = PrimPlayer_government_movement 
				clear_on_owner_change = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "nations.6.a"	
		trigger = { 
			NOT = { has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_exploding_planet }
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_federation_code message = yes }
		country_event = { id = PrimPlay_situation_event.3 days = 30 }
		capital_scope = {   
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = PrimPlayer_government_movement 
				clear_on_owner_change = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "nations.6.authoritarian"	
		trigger = { 
			is_authoritarian = yes 
			NOT = { has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_exploding_planet }
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_federation_code message = yes }
		country_event = { id = PrimPlay_situation_event.3 days = 30 }
		capital_scope = {

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.11201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.11200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain_event }
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.11200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.11205 # GREETINGS
				nations.11210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.11211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.11215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.11220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.11235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.11240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.11245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.11250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.11255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.11260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.11270 # Hire Armies
				nations.11275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.11280 # Patron Panel
				nations.11285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.11205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: GREETINGS
# This = player country; fromfrom = Fanatic Nation
country_event = {
	id = nations.11205
	title = "nations.11205.title"
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = no
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
			}
		}
		text = "nations.11205.desc"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = yes
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
			}
		}
		text = "nations.11205.desc.nomad"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_hostile = fromfrom
		}
		text = "nations.11205.desc.hostile"
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes
	show_sound = event_mysterious_signal
	# custom_gui = "enclave_mercenary_window"
	# custom_gui_option = "enclave_mercenary_option"
	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = fromfrom
		planet_background = fromfrom
		graphical_culture = fromfrom
		city_level = fromfrom
		room = personality_democratic_crusaders_room
	}
	location = root.capital_scope

	immediate = {
		
	}

	# Sue for Peace
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.i"
		trigger = {
			is_hostile = fromfrom
			is_homicidal = no
		}
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = {
				resource = energy
				value >= 2500
			}
		}
		add_resource = {
			energy = -2500
		}
		# hidden_effect = {
			fromfrom = {
				set_faction_hostility = {
					target = root
					set_neutral = yes
				}
			}
		#}
	}

	# Hire fleet
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.a"
		trigger = {
			always = no

		}
		allow = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = nations.11205.tooltip.a
				# event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = {
				#	NOT = {
				#		
				#	}
				#}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = nations.11205.tooltip.b
				
			}
		}
		ai_trade_facility = {
			custom_tooltip = mercenary_tt_hirefleet
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = nations.11210 }
			}
		}
	}
	# Manage hired fleet (extend loan, recall)
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.b"
		custom_tooltip = mercenary_tt_managefleet
		trigger = {
			always = no
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
				is_homicidal = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = nations.11215 }
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Strike Debriefing
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.c"
		allow = { has_technology = tech_missiles_1 }
		custom_tooltip = nations.11205.c.tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = nations.11220 }
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.11205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian }
		#allow = { fromfrom = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { fromfrom = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		fromfrom = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_authoritarian_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		# hidden_effect = {
		#	country_event = { id = nations.11240 }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.11205.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.11205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_authoritarian }
		allow = { fromfrom = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.11220
	title = "nations.11220.title"
	desc = "nations.11220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.11215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.20205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.11225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.11205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# LAUNCHED NUCLEAR STRIKE ON: authoritarian
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.11225
	title = "nations.11225.title"
	picture = GFX_evt_nuclear_explosion
	show_sound = event_air_raid_siren
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { NOT = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		text = "nations.11225.desc.nuclear_winter_avoided"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
		}
		text = "nations.11225.desc.nuclear_winter_situation"
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	immediate = { 
		random_list = {
			0 = {

	# Nuclear Winter Situation - ethic_fanatic_authoritarian Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.11225.option.nuclear_winter_situation"
		trigger = {
			root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { always = yes }	#Cog Note - If countries still exist after nuclear winter, please disable this
			country_event = { id = nations.6 days = 30 } #Unify World Situation
		}
		capital_scope = {
			PrimPlayer_nuclear_winter_planet_setup = yes
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		if = {	
			limit = { 
				always = yes	#Cog Note - Nuclear Winter sounds very interesting as a unique Unify World Situation, let's have all nations be wiped out.
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_communications = root
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 8 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 5 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 2 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Atleast one country survives that was not targeted
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter AVOIDED - ethic_fanatic_authoritarian Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.11225.option.nuclear_winter_avoided"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } } }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_planet_devastation = 13 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.11260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11271_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11272 } # Cultural Exchange
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11272_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11273 } # Fatal Accident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11273_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11274 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11274_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.11277 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_11277_flag } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = nations.11261
	title = "nations.11261.name"
	desc = "nations.11261.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_underground_civilization
	show_sound = event_structural_collapse
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_11261_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.11261.a
		add_deposit = d_sinkhole_subterraneans
		random_planet_army = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = planet.owner
			}
			remove_army = yes
		}
	}
}

# Survivor Pocket
country_event = {
	id = nations.11264
	title = "nations.11264.name"
	desc = "nations.11264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_11264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.11264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.11264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.11264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

	desc = "nations.11268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_11268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.11268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.11268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

		}

# Confederate
country_event = {
	id = nations.11277
	title = "nations.11277.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.11277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_authoritarian = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_11277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.11277.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian }
		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_authoritarian_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_authoritarian_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.11277.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_authoritarian }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_authoritarian_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_authoritarian_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Different
	option = {
		name = "nations.11277.merge.different"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.different.tooltip"
		trigger = { 
			is_authoritarian = no
		}
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_authoritarian_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_authoritarian_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.11277.no
		
	}
	
}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.12201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.12200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.12200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.12205 # GREETINGS
				nations.12210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.12211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.12215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.12220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.12235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.12240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.1212245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.12250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.12255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.12260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.12270 # Hire Armies
				nations.12275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.12280 # Patron Panel
				nations.12285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.12205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: GREETINGS
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.12205
	title = "nations.12205.title"
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = no
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
			}
		}
		text = "nations.12205.desc"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = yes
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
			}
		}
		text = "nations.12205.desc.nomad"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			is_hostile = fromfrom
		}
		text = "nations.12205.desc.hostile"
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes
	show_sound = event_mysterious_signal
	# custom_gui = "enclave_mercenary_window"
	# custom_gui_option = "enclave_mercenary_option"
	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = fromfrom
		planet_background = fromfrom
		graphical_culture = fromfrom
		city_level = fromfrom
		room = personality_ruthless_capitalists_room
	}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.12220
	title = "nations.12220.title"
	desc = "nations.12220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfrom
		# city_level = fromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.12215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.12205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.12220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.12225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.12220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.12205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Atleast one country survives that was not targeted
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter AVOIDED - ethic_fanatic_egalitarian Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.12225.option.nuclear_winter_avoided"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } } }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_planet_devastation = 13 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.12260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12271_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12272 } # Cultural Exchange
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12272_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12273 } # Fatal Accident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12273_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12274 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12274_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.12277 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_12277_flag } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = nations.12264
	title = "nations.12264.name"
	desc = "nations.12264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_12264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.12264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.12264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.12264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.12268
	title = "nations.12268.name"
	desc = "nations.12268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_12268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.12268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.12268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.13201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.13200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.13200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.13205 # GREETINGS
				nations.13210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.13211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.13215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.13220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.13235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.13240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.13245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.13250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.13255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.13260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.13270 # Hire Armies
				nations.13275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.13280 # Patron Panel
				nations.13285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.13205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.13220
	title = "nations.13220.title"
	desc = "nations.13220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root.capital_scope.army.Leader
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.13215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.13205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.13220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.13225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.13220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.13205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.13220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = nations.13230
	title = "nations.13230.title"
	desc = "nations.13230.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root.capital_scope.army.Leader
		planet_background = this
		# graphical_culture = this
		# city_level = this
		room = default_room
	}

	option = {		# Invade
		name = "nations.13230.option.invade"
		trigger = { 
			hidden_trigger = {
				any_country = {
					NOT = { is_hostile = root }
					# has_communications = root
					# has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
					# OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_invasion@root }
				}
			}
		}
		allow = { 
			capital_scope = { has_army = yes }
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = nations.war_room.option.invade.no_truce
				any_country = {
					NOT = { is_hostile = root }
					# has_communications = root
					# has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
					# OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
					NOT = { has_truce = root }
				}
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				# has_communications = root
				# has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
				# OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
			}
			set_faction_hostility = {
				target = root
				set_hostile = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_invasion@root
		}
		# hidden_effect = {
		#	country_event = { id = nations.132035 days = 3600 }
		#}
	}

	option = {		# Instruct high command to prepare a debriefing
		name = "nations.13230.option.debriefing"
		trigger = { 
			hidden_trigger = {
				any_country = {
					NOT = { is_hostile = root }
					# has_communications = root
					# has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
					# OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_invasion@root }
				}
			}
		}
		allow = { 
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = "nations.war_room.option.debriefing.fail_text"
				any_spynetwork = { 
					has_spynetwork_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
					OR = { 
						has_modifier = gather_information_infiltration_5
						has_modifier = gather_information_infiltration_10
						has_modifier = gather_information_infiltration_15
						has_modifier = gather_information_infiltration_20
					}
				}
			}
			has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 500 } 
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -500 }
		# hidden_effect = {
		#	country_event = { id = nations.132035 days = 3600 }
		#}
	}

	option = {		# Share the latest War Report	
		name = "nations.13230.option.war_report"
		allow = { 
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = "nations.war_room.option.war_report.need_war"
				any_country = { 
					is_hostile = root
					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				fail_text = "nations.war_room.option.war_report.need_gather_information"

					has_spynetwork_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root

		name = "nations.13230.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {

				any_country = {
					NOT = { is_hostile = root }
					# has_communications = root
					# has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
					# OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root
				}

					has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation@root

	id = nations.13260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_chain

		NOR = {

		}

country_event = {
	id = nations.13264
	title = "nations.13264.name"
	desc = "nations.13264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_13264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.13264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.13264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.13264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

#Incident
country_event = {
	id = nations.13268
	title = "nations.13268.name"
	desc = "nations.13268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_13268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.13268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.13268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			hidden_effect = { carrier_event = { id = cyber.7010 } }
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }

		}

		hidden_effect = {

		}
	}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.14201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.14200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.14200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.14205 # GREETINGS
				nations.14210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.14211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.14215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.14220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.14235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.14240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.14245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.14250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.14255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.14260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.14270 # Hire Armies
				nations.14275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.14280 # Patron Panel
				nations.14285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.14205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.14220
	title = "nations.14220.title"
	desc = "nations.14220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.14215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.14205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.14220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.14225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.14220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.14205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.14220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# LAUNCHED NUCLEAR STRIKE ON: xenophile
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.14225
	title = "nations.14225.title"
	picture = GFX_evt_nuclear_explosion
	show_sound = event_air_raid_siren
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { NOT = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		text = "nations.14225.desc.nuclear_winter_avoided"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
		}
		text = "nations.14225.desc.nuclear_winter_situation"
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	immediate = { 
		random_list = {
			0 = {
				root = { capital_scope = { change_pc = { class = pc_nuked inherit_entity = no } } }
				#Cog Note - Strategic Asset Espionage mission is key to reducing nuclear winter
				modifier = {
					factor = 0	
					root = { is_nomadic = yes }	#Cog Note - Nomads can't have tomb worlds
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30	#Cog Note - Self Defence in Nuclear Strike
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8

					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root

					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }

					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8

					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root

					}
				}

					}

					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_green
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_socialism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
					}
				}
			}
			4 = { 
				# No mutually assured destruction
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter Situation - ethic_fanatic_xenophile Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.14225.option.nuclear_winter_situation"
		trigger = {
			root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { always = yes }	#Cog Note - If countries still exist after nuclear winter, please disable this
			country_event = { id = nations.6 days = 30 } #Unify World Situation
		}
		capital_scope = {
			PrimPlayer_nuclear_winter_planet_setup = yes
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		if = {	
			limit = { 
				always = yes	#Cog Note - Nuclear Winter sounds very interesting as a unique Unify World Situation, let's have all nations be wiped out.
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_communications = root
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 8 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 5 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 2 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Atleast one country survives that was not targeted
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter AVOIDED - ethic_fanatic_xenophile Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.14225.option.nuclear_winter_avoided"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } } }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_planet_devastation = 13 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.14260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14271_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14272 } # Cultural Exchange
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14272_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.14273 } # Fatal Accident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14273_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {

						}
					}

						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_14277_flag } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = nations.14264
	title = "nations.14264.name"
	desc = "nations.14264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_14264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.14264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.14264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.14264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.14268
	title = "nations.14268.name"
	desc = "nations.14268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_14268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.14268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.14268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.14272
	title = "nations.14272.name"
	desc = "nations.14272.desc"

	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_migration_access = from
			has_commercial_pact = from
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_14272_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.14272.a
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				is_egalitarian = yes
				is_xenophile = yes
				always = yes
			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {
			who = FROM
			status = no
			forced = 10
		}
		random_owned_planet = {
			trigger = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_modifier = { modifier = "planet_luxuries_boost" days = 3600 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.14272.b

			NOR = {
				is_authoritarian = yes
				is_xenophobe = yes
			}

			who = FROM
			status = yes
			forced = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.14272.b
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				is_authoritarian = yes
				is_xenophobe = yes
			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {

# Fatal Accident
country_event = {
	id = nations.14273
	title = "nations.14273.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.14273.desc"
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = no
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = "nations.14273.nomad"
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = yes
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_barely_contained
	show_sound = event_planetary_riot_03
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_owned_colony = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_14273_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.14273.a
		add_favor = {
			target = FROM
			value = 1
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.14273.b
		if = { 
			limit = { is_nomadic = yes }
			add_resource = { alloys = 300 }
		}
		else = {
			add_resource = { minerals = 300 }
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.14273.c
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			hidden_effect = { carrier_event = { id = cyber.7010 } }
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.14273.civic_fanatic_purifiers
		trigger = {
			has_valid_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_owned_planet = { 
				limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
				remove_carrier_flag = genesis_guided_pre_sapient_planet
			}
		}
	}
	
}

# Confederate
country_event = {
	id = nations.14277
	title = "nations.14277.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.14277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_xenophile = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_14277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.14277.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile }
		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_xenophile_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_egalitarian_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.14277.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_xenophile }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_xenophile_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_egalitarian_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Different
	option = {
		name = "nations.14277.merge.different"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.different.tooltip"
		trigger = { 
			is_xenophile = no
		}
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_xenophile_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {

		#}

	}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.15201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.15200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_militarist_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.15200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_militarist_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.15205 # GREETINGS
				nations.15210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.15211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.15215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.15220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.15235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.15240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.15245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.15250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.15255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.15260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.15270 # Hire Armies
				nations.15275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.15280 # Patron Panel
				nations.15285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.15205 }
		}
	}
}
