country_event = {
	id = nations.18264
	title = "nations.18264.name"
	desc = "nations.18264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.18264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.18264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.18264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.18268
	title = "nations.18268.name"
	desc = "nations.18268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.18268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.18268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18270_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.18270.a
		trigger = { 
			OR = { 
				always = yes
				is_materialist = yes
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult_machine
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_machine_empire = no
						is_individual_machine = no
					}
					has_civic = civic_machine_assimilator
				}
			}
			add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_integrated_cybernetics progress = 0.12 }
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_innovations message = yes }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.18270.b
		trigger = { 
			OR = { 
				always = yes
				is_materialist = yes
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult_machine
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_machine_empire = no
						is_individual_machine = no
					}
					has_civic = civic_machine_assimilator
				}
			}
			add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_integrated_cybernetics progress = 0.12 }
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_innovations message = yes }
		set_policy = { policy = trade_policy option = trade_policy_innovation cooldown = yes }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.18270.spiritualist
		trigger = {
			is_spiritualist = yes
			NOR = { 
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult
				has_valid_civic = civic_machine_cult_machine
			}
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_innovations message = yes }
	}
	
}

# Cultural Exchange
country_event = {
	id = nations.18272
	title = "nations.18272.name"
	desc = "nations.18272.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_hand_shake
	show_sound = event_administrative_work_03 #event_conversation
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_migration_access = from
			has_commercial_pact = from
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18272_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.18272.a
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				is_egalitarian = yes
				is_xenophile = yes
				always = yes
			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {
			who = FROM
			status = no
			forced = 10
		}
		random_owned_planet = {
			trigger = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_modifier = { modifier = "planet_luxuries_boost" days = 3600 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.18272.b
		trigger = { 
			NOR = {
				is_authoritarian = yes
				is_xenophobe = yes
			}
		}
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {
			who = FROM
			status = yes
			forced = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.18272.b
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				is_authoritarian = yes
				is_xenophobe = yes
			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {
			who = FROM
			status = yes
			forced = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}

# Fatal Accident
country_event = {
	id = nations.18273
	title = "nations.18273.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.18273.desc"
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = no
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = "nations.18273.nomad"
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = yes
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_settlement
	show_sound = event_structural_collapse
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		always = no #Disabled until story/desc is added
		any_owned_colony = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18273_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.18273.a
		add_favor = {
			target = FROM
			value = 1
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.18273.b
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_planet_devastation = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_displeased

# Confederate
country_event = {
	id = nations.18277
	title = "nations.18277.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.18277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_materialist = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_18277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.18277.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist }
		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_materialist_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_materialist_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.18277.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_materialist }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_materialist_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_materialist_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Different
	option = {
		name = "nations.18277.merge.different"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.different.tooltip"
		trigger = { 
			is_materialist = no
		}
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_materialist_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_materialist_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.18277.no
		
	}
	
}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.19201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.19200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event	#Green Eccocentric
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.19200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_green_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.19205 # GREETINGS
				nations.19210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.19211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.19215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.19220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.19235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.19240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.19245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.19250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.19255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.19260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.19270 # Hire Armies
				nations.19275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.19280 # Patron Panel
				nations.19285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.19205 }
		}
	}
}

			}

	}

			NOR = {

			}

	}

		}

					target = root
					set_neutral = yes
				}
			}
		#}
	}

		name = "nations.19205.a"
		trigger = {
			always = no

		}

				#	}
				#}

				fail_text = nations.19205.tooltip.b
				
			}
		}

			custom_tooltip = mercenary_tt_hirefleet
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = nations.19210 }
			}
		}
	}
	# Manage hired fleet (extend loan, recall)
	option = {
		name = "nations.19205.b"
		custom_tooltip = mercenary_tt_managefleet
		trigger = {
			always = no
			NOR = {
				is_hostile = fromfrom
				is_homicidal = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = nations.19215 }
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Strike Debriefing
	option = {
		name = "nations.19205.c"
		allow = { has_technology = tech_missiles_1 }
		custom_tooltip = "nations.18205.c.tooltip"
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = nations.19220 }
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.19205.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.19205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_green }

		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"

		#	country_event = { id = nations.19240 }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity

		name = "nations.19205.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.19205.merge.normal.tooltip"

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.19220
	title = "nations.19220.title"
	desc = "nations.19220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes	
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.19215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.19205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.19220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_green_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.19225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.19220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_green_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.19205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_green_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.19260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19271_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19272 } # Cultural Exchange
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19272_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19273 } # Fatal Accident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19273_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19274 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19274_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.19277 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_19277_flag } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = nations.19264
	title = "nations.19264.name"
	desc = "nations.19264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_19264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.19264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.19264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.19264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.19268
	title = "nations.19268.name"
	desc = "nations.19268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_19268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.19268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_green_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.19268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_green_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

# Confederate
country_event = {
	id = nations.19277
	title = "nations.19277.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.19277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_green = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_19277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {

		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }

		#}
	}

		name = "nations.19277.merge.normal"

		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_green }

		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through

		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Different
	option = {

		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.different.tooltip"
		trigger = { 
			is_green = no
		}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.20201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.20200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.20200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.20205 # GREETINGS
				nations.20210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.20211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.20215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.20220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.20235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.20240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.20245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.20250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.20255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.20260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.20270 # Hire Armies
				nations.20275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.20280 # Patron Panel
				nations.20285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.20205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.20220
	title = "nations.20220.title"
	desc = "nations.20220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes	
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.20215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.20205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.20220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.20225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.20220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.20205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# LAUNCHED NUCLEAR STRIKE ON: industrial
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.20225
	title = "nations.20225.title"
	picture = GFX_evt_nuclear_explosion
	show_sound = event_air_raid_siren
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { NOT = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		text = "nations.20225.desc.nuclear_winter_avoided"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
		}
		text = "nations.20225.desc.nuclear_winter_situation"
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	immediate = { 
		random_list = {
			0 = {
				root = { capital_scope = { change_pc = { class = pc_nuked inherit_entity = no } } }
				#Cog Note - Strategic Asset Espionage mission is key to reducing nuclear winter
				modifier = {
					factor = 0	
					root = { is_nomadic = yes }	#Cog Note - Nomads can't have tomb worlds
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30	#Cog Note - Self Defence in Nuclear Strike
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 16		#Cog Note - Xenopbobes are more likely to launch a nuclear strike
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophobe_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 4 	#Cog Note - Xenophiles are less likely to launch a nuclear strike
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 16	#Cog Note - Militarists are more likely to launch a nuclear strike
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_militarist_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_militarist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 4		#Cog Note - Pacifists are less likely to launch a nuclear strike
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_pacifist_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_pacifist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_spiritualist_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_spiritualist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_materialist_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_green_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_green
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_socialism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
			}
			4 = { 
				# No mutually assured destruction
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter Situation - ethic_fanatic_industrial Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.20225.option.nuclear_winter_situation"
		trigger = {
			root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { always = yes }	#Cog Note - If countries still exist after nuclear winter, please disable this
			country_event = { id = nations.6 days = 30 } #Unify World Situation
		}
		capital_scope = {
			PrimPlayer_nuclear_winter_planet_setup = yes
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		if = {	
			limit = { 
				always = yes	#Cog Note - Nuclear Winter sounds very interesting as a unique Unify World Situation, let's have all nations be wiped out.
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_communications = root
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 8 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 5 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 2 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Atleast one country survives that was not targeted
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter AVOIDED - ethic_fanatic_industrial Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.20225.option.nuclear_winter_avoided"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } } }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_planet_devastation = 13 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.20260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.20268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_20268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.20269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_20269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.20270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_20270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.20271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_20271_flag } }
						}
					}

						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_20272_flag } }

						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event

		}

		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = nations.20264
	title = "nations.20264.name"
	desc = "nations.20264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_20264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.20264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.20264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.20264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.20268
	title = "nations.20268.name"
	desc = "nations.20268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_20268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.20268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.20268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

	}

					is_individual_machine = yes
				}

		}

country_event = {
	id = nations.20277
	title = "nations.20277.name"

	}

			limit = { is_industrial = yes }

			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}

		}

		#}

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.21201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.21200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event

	id = nations.21200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.21205 # GREETINGS
				nations.21210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.21211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.21215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.21220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.21235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.21240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.21245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.21250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.21255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.21260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.21270 # Hire Armies
				nations.21275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.21280 # Patron Panel
				nations.21285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country

				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}

		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.21220
	title = "nations.21220.title"
	desc = "nations.21220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes	
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.21215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.21205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.21220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.21225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.21220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.21205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# Collapse
country_event = {
	id = nations.21260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = { 
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_chain_event 	#Must have initial diplomacy event happen before these events can fire
			has_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event					#Optimizing checks
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21268 } # Incident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21268_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21269 } # Famine
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21269_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21270 } # Gift
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21270_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21271 } # Refugees
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21271_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21272 } # Cultural Exchange
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21272_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21273 } # Fatal Accident
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21273_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {

							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21274_flag } }
						}
					}
					0 = {
						country_event = { id = nations.21277 } # Technology Request
						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_21277_flag } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	}

		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

		}

# Survivor Pocket
country_event = {
	id = nations.21264
	title = "nations.21264.name"
	desc = "nations.21264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_21264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.21264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.21264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.21264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

#Incident
country_event = {
	id = nations.21268
	title = "nations.21268.name"
	desc = "nations.21268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_21268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.21268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.21268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

		text = "nations.21277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_authoritarian = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_21277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.21277.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_socialism }
		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_socialism_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_socialism_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.21277.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_socialism }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_socialism_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"

# Cog Note - You can use this event to force Nation State Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = country being contacted; from = target country (player) #Cog Note - Swapped scopes so successor event works
country_event = {
	id = nations.22201
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { country_event = { id = nations.22200 } }
		FROM = { remove_country_flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain_event }
		FROM = {
			set_timed_country_flag = {
				flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
				days = 360
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave Greetings: Gatekeeper event
# Via on_custom_diplomacy | this = target country (player); from = country being contacted
country_event = {
	id = nations.22200
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
		}
		NOT = {
			has_active_event = {
				nations.22205 # GREETINGS
				nations.22210 # HIRE FLEET
				nations.22211 # THANKS FOR RENTAL
				nations.22215 # MANAGE HIRED FLEET
				nations.22220 # Nuclear Strike Debriefing
				nations.22235 # Expand Mercenary Fleet
				nations.22240 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
				nations.22245 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Khan
				nations.22250 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Gray Tempest
				nations.22255 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Prethoryn
				nations.22260 # Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity Unbidden
				nations.22270 # Hire Armies
				nations.22275 # Merc Pays Back Patron
				nations.22280 # Patron Panel
				nations.22285 # Recall Fleet
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ai_trade_facility = {
			# offer_hire_mercenary_fleet = yes
			# offer_prolong_fleet_contract = yes
			from = {
				# save_event_target_as = mercenary_enclave_country
				# Failsafe to catch if fleets are no longer on loan
				# remove_loaning_fleet_flag = yes
			}
			country_event = { id = nations.22205 }
		}
	}
}

# Mercenary Enclave: Nuclear Strike Debriefing
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.22220
	title = "nations.22220.title"
	desc = "nations.22220.desc"
	is_triggered_only = yes	
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		# portrait = root
		planet_background = fromfromfrom
		# graphical_culture = fromfromfrom
		# city_level = fromfromfrom
		room = observation_post_room
	}
	

	# Discuss another matter (return to menu) - Cog Note, this makes it 5x FROM and game can only do 4x
	# option = {
	#	name = "nations.22215.a"
	#	hidden_effect = {
	#		country_event = { id = nations.22205 }
	#	}
	#}

	# Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.22220.option.nuclear_strike_launched"
		# allow = { # has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 } }
		trigger = {
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		add_resource = { energy = -1000 }
		custom_tooltip = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike_trust
		hidden_effect = {
			fromfromfrom = {
				add_trust = {
					who = root
					amount = -100
				}
				set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@ROOT
			}
			set_country_flag = PrimPlayer_launch_nuclear_strike@fromfromfrom
			country_event = { id = nations.22225 }
		}
	}
	# Cannot Launch Nuclear Strike
	option = {
		name = "nations.22220.option.nuclear_strike_blocked"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount < 1000 }
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level <= 50
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# country_event = { id = nations.22205 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = nations.option.nuclear_strike_blocked.reason
	}

	option = {
		name = "nations.11220.option.goodbye"
		trigger = {
			# has_country_resource = { type = energy amount >= 1000 }
			has_spynetwork = yes
			any_spynetwork = {
				TARGET = { has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root }
				is_spynetwork_level > 50
			}
		}
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

# LAUNCHED NUCLEAR STRIKE ON: capitalism
# This = player country; event_target:mercenary_enclave_country = enclave country
country_event = {
	id = nations.22225
	title = "nations.22225.title"
	picture = GFX_evt_nuclear_explosion
	show_sound = event_air_raid_siren
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { NOT = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		text = "nations.22225.desc.nuclear_winter_avoided"
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked }
		}
		text = "nations.22225.desc.nuclear_winter_situation"
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	immediate = { 
		random_list = {
			0 = {
				root = { capital_scope = { change_pc = { class = pc_nuked inherit_entity = no } } }
				#Cog Note - Strategic Asset Espionage mission is key to reducing nuclear winter
				modifier = {
					factor = 0	
					root = { is_nomadic = yes }	#Cog Note - Nomads can't have tomb worlds
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 30	#Cog Note - Self Defence in Nuclear Strike
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_egalitarian_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root

						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}

					add = 4 	#Cog Note - Xenophiles are less likely to launch a nuclear strike
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_xenophile_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophile
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }

					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_green
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_industrial_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_socialism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_socialism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 8
					root = { capital_scope = { has_deposit = d_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_blocker } }
					any_country = { 
						has_communications = root
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
						OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
						has_technology = tech_missiles_1
						NOT = { has_country_flag = espionage_operation_targeted_by@root }
					}
				}
			}
			4 = { 
				# No mutually assured destruction
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter Situation - ethic_fanatic_capitalism Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.22225.option.nuclear_winter_situation"
		trigger = {
			root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { always = yes }	#Cog Note - If countries still exist after nuclear winter, please disable this
			country_event = { id = nations.6 days = 30 } #Unify World Situation
		}
		capital_scope = {
			PrimPlayer_nuclear_winter_planet_setup = yes
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		if = {	
			limit = { 
				always = yes	#Cog Note - Nuclear Winter sounds very interesting as a unique Unify World Situation, let's have all nations be wiped out.
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
					has_communications = root
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 8 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 5 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 
				root = { capital_scope = { count_deposits = { category = deposit_cat_nation_blocker count > 2 } } }
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_communications = root
					OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
				}
				destroy_country = yes
				nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			# Atleast one country survives that was not targeted
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
	# Nuclear Winter AVOIDED - ethic_fanatic_socialism Is Destroyed
	option = {
		name = "nations.22225.option.nuclear_winter_avoided"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { root = { capital_scope = { is_planet_class = pc_nuked } } }
		}
		capital_scope = { add_planet_devastation = 13 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			destroy_country = yes
			nuclear_winter_clear_nation_deposit_flag = yes
		}
		# Rest of world distrusts player
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_communications = root
				OR = { is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation is_country_type = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_nomadic }
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = root
				amount = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

#Establish Branch
country_event = {
	id = nations.90002
	title = "nations.90002.branch_office.title"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = from
		planet_background = from
		graphical_culture = from
		city_level = from
		room = personality_ruthless_capitalists_room
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	desc = {
		text = "nations.90002.branch_office.desc"
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
				root.capital_scope = { NOT = { has_branch_office = Prev } }
			}
		}
	}
	
	desc = {
		text = "nations.90002.branch_office.desc.established"
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
				root.capital_scope = { has_branch_office = Prev }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "nations.90002.branch_office.yes"
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
				root.capital_scope = { NOT = { has_branch_office = Prev } }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_authoritarian_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
		from = {
			root.capital_scope = { establish_branch_office = PREV }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "nations.90002.branch_office.no"
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
				root.capital_scope = { NOT = { has_branch_office = Prev } }
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "nations.90002.branch_office.established"
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_country_flag = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation@root
				root.capital_scope = { has_branch_office = Prev }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = nations.22260
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain

	hide_window = yes
	
	trigger = {
		NOR = {

		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			owner = { 
				random_list = {
					0 = {

						modifier = {
							add = 1
							prev = { NOT = { has_situation_flag = nations_22268_flag } }
						}
					}

						country_event = { id = nations.22269 } # Famine

							add = 1

					}

country_event = {
	id = nations.22264
	title = "nations.22264.name"
	desc = "nations.22264.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_in_the_dark
	show_sound = event_scanner
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22264_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		create_species = {
			name = random
			class = random_non_machine
			portrait = random
			traits = random
			homeworld = planet
		}
		last_created_species = {
			save_event_target_as = subterranean_species@root.owner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.22264.a
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = no
				}
			}
			text = "nations.22264.b"
		}
		name = {
			trigger = {
				owner = {
					is_subterranean_empire = yes
				}
			}
			text = "nations.22264.b.subterranean"
		}
		trigger = {
			#free_housing > 500
			owner = { OR = { is_authoritarian = yes is_xenophobe = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = PrimPlayer_situation } }	#Provides Subterranean Origin Outcome
					owner = { set_country_flag = origin_subterranean_population_adopted }
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = event_target:subterranean_species@root.owner
				ethos = {
					ethic = "ethic_spiritualist"
				}
			}
			event_target:subterranean_species_pop_group@root.owner = {
				set_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery cooldown = yes }
			}
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = nations.22268
	title = "nations.22268.name"
	desc = "nations.22268.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_smear_campaign
	show_sound = event_burning_city
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_envoy = {
			OR = {
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = improve_relations
					target = FROM
				}
				has_envoy_task = {
					task = harm_relations
					target = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22268_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = nations.22268.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value >= 100 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			influence = -100
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_envoy_insult_taken who = root } }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.22268.b
		random_envoy = {
			limit = { 
				OR = {
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = improve_relations
						target = FROM
					}
					has_envoy_task = {
						task = harm_relations
						target = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			kill_leader = {
				show_notification = yes
				fire = no
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

}

		years_passed > 11	#This is a rough one to get early
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22269_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_famine_event
			days = 2520
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.22269.a
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = food value >= 1000 }
		}
		add_resource = {
			food = -1000
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_pleased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.22269.b
		trigger = { resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = food value >= 1000 } }
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nations.22269.c
		trigger = { resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = food value < 1000 } }
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}
	
}

country_event = {
	id = nations.22272
	title = "nations.22272.name"
	desc = "nations.22272.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_hand_shake
	show_sound = event_administrative_work_03 #event_conversation
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_migration_access = from
			has_commercial_pact = from
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22272_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.22272.a
		trigger = { 
			OR = {
				is_egalitarian = yes
				is_xenophile = yes
				always = yes
			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = @tier3researchreward
			min = @tier3researchmin
			max = @tier3researchmax
		}
		set_closed_borders = {
			who = FROM
			status = no
			forced = 10
		}
		random_owned_planet = {
			trigger = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			add_modifier = { modifier = "planet_luxuries_boost" days = 3600 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_pleased
