			forced = 10
		}
		custom_tooltip = ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_displeased
		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_down who = ROOT } }
		}
	}

			OR = {

			}
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {

			is_nomadic = no
		}
	}

		text = "nations.22273.nomad"
		trigger = {
			is_nomadic = yes
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_burning_settlement
	show_sound = event_structural_collapse
	location = ROOT
	#event_chain = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_capitalism_nation_chain
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		always = no #Disabled until story/desc is added
		any_owned_colony = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22273_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nations.22273.a
		add_favor = {
			target = FROM
			value = 1
		}
		random_owned_planet = { 
			limit = { has_deposit = d_PrimPlayer_nation_deposit }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = "PrimPlayer_angry_population"
					days = 3600

		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = PrimPlayer_opinion_flavors_up who = ROOT } }
		}

# Confederate
country_event = {
	id = nations.22277
	title = "nations.22277.name"
	desc = {
		text = "nations.22277.desc"
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	diplomatic = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = FROM
		planet_background = FROM
		graphical_culture = FROM
		city_level = FROM
		room = FROM
	}
	location = root.capital_scope
	
	trigger = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_authoritarian = yes }
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } }
		}
		else = {
			FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from.from = { set_situation_flag = nations_22277_flag }
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = PrimPlayer_ethic_fanatic_nation_chain_event
			days = 360
		}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Fanatic Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.22277.merge.fanatic"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.fanatic.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism }
		#allow = { FROM = { opinion = { who = root value >= 150 } } }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_capitalism_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_capitalism_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}

	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Normal Similarity
	option = {
		name = "nations.22277.merge.normal"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.normal.tooltip"
		trigger = { has_ethic = ethic_capitalism }
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= good } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_capitalism_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_capitalism_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	# Merge Nations Event Chain - Different
	option = {
		name = "nations.22277.merge.different"
		#custom_tooltip = "nations.12205.merge.different.tooltip"
		trigger = { 
			is_capitalism = no
		}
		allow = { FROM = { opinion_level = { who = root level >= excellent } } }
		FROM = { destroy_country = yes } #Placeholder effect. Ideally we have a situation to play this through
		capital_scope = { set_carrier_flag = PrimPlayer_capitalism_union_situation_complete }
		custom_tooltip = "nations_improve_relations_tooltip"
		#hidden_effect = {
		#	start_situation = { type = PrimPlayer_ethic_capitalism_union_situation target = root }
		#}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = nations.22277.no
		
	}
	
}

		name = "nations.90001.open_border.no"
		trigger = { from = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_industrial } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "nations.90001.open_border.no"
		trigger = { from = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_socialism } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "nations.90001.open_border.no"
		trigger = { from = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_capitalism } }
	}
